# mk64: boot -> Mario GP 50cc Mushroom Cup, all 4 races driven by a closed-loop pilot (pure pursuit on a recorded AI kart path; gPlayers 0x800F6990 from the n64decomp/mk64 decomp), recorded as plain pad inputs. NO cheats/pokes.
# Frame counts are VI fields (60/s NTSC). See compat/README.md for the commands.
run 120
screenshot boot_2s
run 480
screenshot boot_10s
run 600
screenshot title_20s
pulse START 60 20       # Start pressed 3 times over 1 s (some titles ignore the first press)
run 570
screenshot after_start_10s
pulse START 60 20       # again: gets past "press start" menus that need two presses
press A 5 25
run 540
screenshot after_start_20s
# [cert_scripts3] Game Select (1P, Mario GP 50cc) -> OK -> character -> cup -> race
